Removing the official answer as well collapses reliability (ICC 0.888 to 0.628), inflates scores, and makes judge reasoning effort matter again. The rubric is what decouples grading from judge intelligence, and within the rubric the official answer does nearly all the work. We find no evidence of length preference or same-family preference under rubric-anchored grading.

</details>


### [131] [Do Large Language Models Play Six Degrees of Separation? Measuring Topological Compression in Long-Context Manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17950)
*Md. Faiyaz Abdullah Sayeedi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文绕过注意力权重，直接分析大语言模型隐状态流形的动态几何结构，证明深层LLM潜在空间天然组织成小世界网络。研究发现深层推理层将不相干的语义锚点压缩为高度可导航的路径，严格受限于"六度分隔"（≤6个语义跳数）。该框架还被应用于RAG零样本幻觉检测，事实有据的生成与源上下文的拓扑距离约为3跳，而幻觉会引发严重的拓扑坍缩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的基于注意力的可解释性方法因注意力汇聚（attention sinks）等路由伪影，无法真实捕捉语义邻近性。论文旨在绕过注意力权重，从隐状态流形的几何结构层面直接理解LLM如何实现长上下文多跳推理，并探索其作为事实可靠性评估工具的可能性。

Method: 先将长上下文表示的高维连续相似度矩阵稀疏化为无权图，在不同架构上追踪高度不连通的语义锚点间的连通性，从而将LLM潜在空间建模为网络并分析其小世界特性。随后将该几何框架应用于RAG场景中的零样本幻觉检测，通过比较生成内容与源上下文之间的语义跳数来评估事实性。

Result: 发现早期句法层完全碎片化，而深层推理层出现尖锐的拓扑相变，将大规模概念距离压缩为高度可导航的路径且严格满足六度分隔上限（≤6跳）。在RAG幻觉检测中，事实有据的生成与源上下文保持约3跳的结构完整性，而幻觉导致严重的拓扑坍缩。

Conclusion: 该工作从数学上形式化了Transformer执行抽象推理的内在机制，为评估事实可靠性提供了一种全新的纯几何信号，不依赖注意力权重，具有跨架构适用性和实用检测价值。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) demonstrate remarkable multi-hop reasoning capabilities over long contexts, yet the internal mechanisms enabling these distant cognitive leaps remain poorly understood. Traditional attention-based interpretability often fails to capture true semantic proximity due to routing artifacts like attention sinks. In this paper, we bypass attention weights to directly analyze the dynamic geometry of the hidden state manifold, proving that deep LLM latent spaces natively organize into Small-World networks. By sparsifying the continuous similarity matrices of long-context representations into unweighted graphs, we trace the connectivity between highly disjoint semantic anchors across two distinct architectures. Our findings reveal a sharp topological phase transition: while early syntactic layers remain entirely fractured, deep reasoning layers abruptly compress massive conceptual distances into highly navigable pathways strictly bounded by the "Six Degrees of Separation" limit (=< 6 semantic hops). Furthermore, we demonstrate the practical efficacy of this framework by applying it to zero-shot hallucination detection within Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) using the RAGognize dataset. We show that factually grounded generations maintain structural integrity with their source context (approximately 3 hops), whereas hallucinations induce severe topological collapse. Ultimately, this work mathematically formalizes how transformers execute abstract reasoning and provides a novel, strictly geometric signature for evaluating factual reliability.

</details>


### [132] [When Writing Style Drifts: Benchmarking Authorship Verification under Distribution Shifts in Genre, Time and the AI-Era](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17979)
*Lotta Kiefer,Brisca Balthes,Christoph Leiter,Yamen Ajjour,Elena Schmidt,Steffen Eger*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了AVShift，这是首个用于德语作者身份验证的基准数据集，包含超过15万对文本，覆盖三个体裁和21年时间跨度，用于在统一框架下系统评估跨体裁、时间跨度和AI时代三种分布偏移对作者身份验证的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的作者身份验证基准通常孤立地研究分布偏移因素且主要关注英语，缺乏对模型在真实条件下鲁棒性的理解。作者希望通过构建多因素联合评估的德语基准来填补这一空白。

Method: 构建AVShift基准，包含15万+文本对、三个体裁、21年时间跨度，在统一框架下评估跨体裁、时间跨度和AI时代偏移；对基于特征、嵌入和LLM的方法进行基准测试，并进行风格特征分析。

Result: 微调的LLM在跨体裁泛化方面表现最佳，且受益于风格多样的训练数据；时间漂移是影响AV最强的因素之一，文档时间间隔越大性能下降越显著；未发现AVShift中存在可测量的AI时代分布偏移；发现部分风格特征跨体裁保持稳定，但其相对重要性因体裁转换而异。

Conclusion: AVShift为德语作者身份验证的多重分布偏移研究提供了统一评测框架，为理解模型鲁棒性及风格特征稳定性提供了重要洞见，数据和代码已公开供后续研究使用。

Abstract: Authorship verification (AV) assumes that an author's writing style remains sufficiently stable to distinguish it from that of other writers. In practice, however, this assumption is challenged by distribution shifts caused by changes in genre, time, and AI-assisted writing. Existing AV benchmarks typically study these factors in isolation and focus predominantly on English, limiting our understanding of model robustness under realistic conditions. We introduce AVShift, the first German benchmark for systematically evaluating AV under multiple distribution shifts. AVShift comprises over 150,000 text pairs spanning three genres and 21 years, enabling controlled evaluation of cross-genre, temporal, and AI-era shifts within a unified framework. We benchmark representative feature-based, embedding-based, and LLM-based approaches. Our experiments show that fine-tuned LLMs generalize best across genres and benefit substantially from stylistically diverse training data. We further demonstrate that temporal drift is one of the strongest factors affecting AV, with performance degrading significantly as the time gap between documents increases. In contrast, we find no evidence of a measurable AI-era distribution shift within AVShift. Finally, our feature analysis reveals stylistic features that remain stable across genres, while their relative importance varies depending on the specific genre transition. We release AVShift and our code for future research.

</details>


### [133] [Judge, Retrieve, or Abstain: Uncertainty-Guarded LLM Judging with Provable Risk Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17994)
*Sher Badshah,Ali Emami,Hassan Sajjad*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Using LLMs as judges has become standard practice for evaluating model outputs at scale. This is particularly common for subjective, open-ended tasks such as assessing helpfulness or alignment, where no single reference answer exists. However, objective tasks introduce a distinct reliability challenge for reference-free LLM judging. In the absence of a reference answer, the judge evaluates factual correctness either through its parametric knowledge or through tool augmentation. Although the former enables efficient evaluation, the judge may hallucinate or lack sufficient evidence for its verdict. Conversely, tool augmentation can provide additional evidence but introduces extra computational cost and requires an appropriate mechanism to determine when and how that evidence should be used reliably. More importantly, neither approach alone provides formal control over the risk of accepted verdicts or guarantees their reliability at a specified level. We propose a risk-controlled framework that calibrates uncertainty thresholds on a held-out set so that the false discovery rate among accepted verdicts remains below a user-specified level~$α$ with high probability, using finite-sample Clopper--Pearson intervals. When the parametric mode is not sufficiently confident, the instance is routed to a retrieval-augmented mode, where the judge gathers web evidence and re-evaluates the instance under a second calibrated threshold. The finite-sample guarantee carries over to this two-threshold routing without additional assumptions. Across open-domain QA benchmarks and judges of varying scales, the framework maintains the target error rate while achieving substantially higher coverage than single-mode baselines.

</details>


### [134] [The IOL-AI Challenge: An Open Challenge towards Advancing Linguistic Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18011)
*Eduardo Sánchez,Rita Berrada,Dan-Mircea Mirea,Sara Rajaee,Alexander Piperski,Ana Meta Dolinar,Boris Iomdin,Andrey Nikulin,Mariya Shmatova,Marzieh Fadaee,Julia Kreutzer*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了IOL-AI挑战赛，一个基于2026年国际语言学奥林匹克竞赛（IOL）个人赛未公开题目的开放科学竞赛。731份提交来自46个团队，在严格计算预算下进行。Claude Opus 4.8获得相当于金牌的评委评分，而资源受限系统的得分接近参赛者底部5%。关键发现包括：能力不由模型规模决定（14B模型超越两倍规模模型），自动评估指标与评委排名一致但压缩了分数差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型的推理研究主要集中在数学和代码等已提供规则规则的领域，而语言学谜题需要模型先发现系统规则再在其中推理，这是一种反向挑战。作者希望通过IOL-AI挑战赛来填补这一空白，评估LLM在需要自主发现语言学规则的任务上的推理能力。

Method: 作者组织了IOL-AI挑战赛，使用IOL 2026个人赛的未公开题目，采用双重评估方式：自动评估和由IOL官方评审团按照人类参赛者的评分标准评分。比赛设置了严格的计算预算（一个T4 GPU，30分钟），此外还基准测试了15个无资源限制的前沿和开放模型。

Result: Claude Opus 4.8获得相当于金牌的评委分数；资源受限系统得分在人类参赛者底部5%范围内。14B参数模型表现优于两倍大小的模型，性能提升主要来自解码和输出处理而非模型容量。自动评估与评委排名一致，但对弱系统高估约13分，低估强系统分数。前沿模型对问题语言的先验知识并不能显著帮助其解决语言学推理任务。

Conclusion: 语言学推理可以作为评估LLM通用推理能力的强替代基准，因为先验知识对解决任务帮助有限，且其挑战性与数学和代码推理不同。自动评估可以作为评审团评分的可靠近似，但需要注意其分数压缩效应。

Abstract: Reasoning in LLMs is overwhelmingly studied in domains that provide a model with rules: mathematics and code. Linguistic puzzles invert this: the solver must first discover the system before reasoning within it. We present the IOL-AI Challenge, an open-science competition run on the unseen problems of the International Linguistics Olympiad (IOL) 2026 Individual Contest, evaluated both automatically and, for the first time, by members of the official IOL Jury under the same rubrics applied to human contestants. The challenge drew 731 submissions from 46 teams under a strict compute budget (one T4, 30 mins). We additionally benchmark 15 unconstrained frontier and open models, with Claude Opus 4.8 earning a jury score equivalent to a gold medal, while both resource-constrained systems we submitted for jury grading scored in the range of the bottom 5% of contestants. Capability was not determined by scale: 14B submissions outperform models twice their size, and gains come from decoding and output-handling rather than model capacity. We also found that automatic metrics rank systems exactly as the jury does, but compress the scale, upscoring weak systems by ~13 points and understating strong ones. Our analysis shows that while frontier models might have prior knowledge about some of the problem languages, it does not significantly help them solve the linguistic reasoning tasks, leaving linguistic reasoning as a strong benchmarking proxy for generalizable reasoning skills.

</details>


### [135] [Chain-of-Experience for Continual LLM Improvement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18027)
*Haoqin Tu,Yunhao Fang,Yizhong Wang,Cihang Xie,Shen Yan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Chain-of-Experience（CoE）范式，研究LLM在测试时通过迭代交互积累经验并持续改进的能力。实验覆盖数学、编程和知识领域，使用8种LLM（包括GPT-5、Gemini-2.5 Pro、Claude-4.5 Sonnet），结果显示CoE相比无反馈基线提升显著，自反馈即可带来5.6%的总体改进和19%的API成本降低，组合互补反馈通道可进一步提升效果，且CoE比现有测试时策略具有更高的每token准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统LLM评估忽视了模型通过推理时交互进行学习的能力，而人类本质上是持续从经验中学习的。作者希望研究LLM在测试时如何通过迭代经验积累实现持续改进，突破零样本推理的局限。

Method: 提出Chain-of-Experience（CoE）设置，模型通过迭代交互（自反馈或环境反馈如正确性、编程测试通过率）积累经验痕迹，形成持续改进循环。使用8种LLM在数学、编程和知识三大领域进行系统性评估。

Result: 迭代经验一致优于无反馈基线；仅自反馈即带来5.6%的总体改进和19%的API成本降低；组合互补反馈通道（模型信号+正确性信号）可获得额外增益；CoE比现有测试时策略具有更高的每token准确率；LLM基础能力与改进能力正相关；在弱或虚假反馈下依然稳健；不同反馈贡献不同改进方面；大部分增益在迭代早期出现。

Conclusion: CoE是一种有效且成本可控的测试时学习范式，不同的反馈机制对应不同的改进路径，且模型在反馈不理想时仍具鲁棒性，为未来设计更高效的测试时交互学习策略提供了基础。

Abstract: Humans continuously learn from experience, whereas conventional large language model (LLM) evaluations ignore the models' ability to improve through inference-time interaction. In this paper, we study how LLMs learn from iterative experience at test time, a setting we refer to as Chain-of-Experience (CoE), where models accumulate experiential traces through iterative interactions with self or environmental feedback to form a continual improvement loop beyond zero-shot inference. We instantiate CoE with diverse feedback mechanisms, including model self-feedback and environmental signals such as correctness or public coding test pass rates, and evaluate across math, coding, and knowledge domains using 8 LLMs, including GPT-5, Gemini-2.5 Pro, Claude-4.5 Sonnet. Our study shows that leveraging iterative experience consistently outperforms feedback-free baselines, achieving substantial gains with self feedback alone, alongside a 5.6% overall improvement and 19% lower API cost across tasks and models. We further show that combining complementary feedback channels (e.g., model and correctness signals) yields additional gains, and that CoE delivers higher accuracy per token than existing test-time strategies. We observe a positive correlation between LLM base ability and improvement capacity, and show that models remain robust under weak or spurious feedback, with different feedback contributing to distinct improvement aspects and most gains emerging early in the iterations.

</details>


### [136] [Language Has Two Parameters: Narrative-Induced Semantic Plasticity and Phase-Sensitive Interpretation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18041)
*Hollis Robbins*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出语言中存在第二个参数"相位"（phase），不同于传统的关联强度（幅度），相位存在于意义之间，决定共同激活的意义如何组合，甚至能逆转意义的贡献。该参数是有符号的、持久的、索引到个体和二元组的。标准Transformer在冻结推理中缺乏相位显式表征，而单义性可解释性方向正好与相位对立。作者提出弱版本主张（解释需要第二个关系参数）和强版本主张（量子概率论约束这些现象）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的词嵌入和注意力权重只能度量词共现的幅度（关联强度），将所有作者的历史聚合成一个总体均值，丢失了个体和二元组的差异。作者认为仅靠幅度无法完整解释语言中反讽、暗示、引语等复杂现象，需要引入第二个参数来解释这些现象。

Method: 论文通过理论论证的方式提出相位参数的概念，识别其数学性质（有符号、持久、个体/二元索引），批判现有Transformer架构和单义性可解释性程序缺乏相位表征，并设计六项可检验的预测实验来验证被抑制意义是否保持活跃、遭遇顺序是否改变短语功能、信号标记是否改变共享短语的理解、以及模型是否被历史改变等问题。

Result: 论文提出六项具体可测试的预测，涵盖被抑制意义的活跃性、遭遇顺序效应、信号标记效应和历史对模型的影响。作者指出量子概率仅是相位的符号表达工具，并非声称大脑存在量子过程。强版本（量子演算约束这些现象）因遭遇顺序约束尚未导出而暂时无法成立。

Conclusion: 论文捍卫的弱版本主张是：语言解释需要第二个关系参数——有符号、持久、索引到个体和二元组的相位参数。理论要求的架构是具备智能体索引和相位承载语义状态的语言模型。这标志着从聚合统计的语义模型向个体化、关系性语义模型的范式转向。

Abstract: Language has two parameters. Count how often words occur together and you estimate amplitude, the strength of association. Word embeddings and attention weights refine that count, which sums every writer in the corpus together. This paper claims a second parameter, phase, which signed weights learned from a corpus do not supply. Phase exists only between meanings: it determines how coactivated meanings combine, and it can reverse what a meaning contributes while that meaning stays fully present. A speaker can set phase in the signal through linguistic form; encounters install phase relations and history distributes them. Population averaging deletes history-indexed phase: agent-deindexed corpora identify the population marginal state and determine no individual or dyadic state, at any scale. The standard transformer has no explicit representation for phase in frozen inference, and the interpretability program measuring progress by monosemanticity is optimizing against it: the coexistence it treats as a defect is the condition of allusion, irony, and quotation. Six predictions test whether a suppressed meaning stays active, whether encounter order changes what a phrase does, whether marking the signal changes how a shared phrase is taken, and whether a model given a history is changed by it or only informed about it. The claim defended is the weak version: interpretation requires a second relational parameter, signed, persistent, and indexed to individuals and dyads. Quantum probability is one notation for the parameter; nothing in the formalism claims quantum processes in the brain. The strong version, that the quantum calculus constrains these phenomena as signed classical models do not, rests on an encounter-order constraint not yet derived. The architecture the theory calls for is a language model with agent-indexed, phase-bearing semantic states.

</details>


### [137] [TokEval: A Tokenizer Evaluation Suite](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18062)
*Clara Meister*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出TokEval，一个超越生育率和压缩率等标准指标的分词器评估框架，引入UTF-8字符边界完整性和数字位值边界对齐等结构敏感指标。通过控制变量的预训练实验，发现信息论指标预测语言建模能力（Spearman ρ高达0.80），而结构敏感指标与任务准确率相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型分词器的选择通常缺乏系统评估，因为人们对哪些分词器属性影响下游性能的哪些方面理解有限。现有的生育率、压缩率等标准指标不足以捕捉对模型能力有影响的结构性特征。

Method: 提出TokEval框架，包含超越传统指标的分词器评估度量（如UTF-8字符边界完整性、数字位值边界对齐）。通过控制变量预训练实验，仅改变分词器的训练数据混合、预分词策略和训练算法，在bits-per-byte和多个基准上评估模型性能，并分析与内在指标的相关性。

Result: 不同内在属性对不同模型能力影响不同：信息论指标（如bits-per-byte）预测语言建模能力（Spearman ρ高达0.80），而结构敏感指标（如数字和换行处理）与任务准确率相关。

Conclusion: TokEval有望实现更有原则性的分词器评估，在内在测量与预训练扫描结果一致的情况下，用前者替代后者，从而降低评估成本并提升分词器选择的合理性。

Abstract: Language model tokenizers are typically selected with minimal evaluation, despite the fact that their design choices directly impact model capabilities. This can be partly attributed to a limited understanding of which tokenizer properties affect which aspects of downstream performance. We introduce TokEval, a framework of tokenizer evaluation metrics that goes beyond standard measures like fertility and compression rate to capture linguistically and structurally meaningful properties, e.g., UTF-8 character boundary integrity and digit place-value boundary alignment for mathematics. To validate whether these metrics are predictive of downstream model performance, we conduct controlled language model pretraining experiments, varying solely the tokenizers' training data mixture, pretokenization strategy, and training algorithm. We evaluate the resulting models on bits-per-byte (a tokenizer-agnostic version of perplexity) and several benchmarks, spanning linguistic understanding, mathematical reasoning, and code generation. Our experiments suggest that different intrinsic properties have different impacts on model abilities: information-theoretic metrics predict language modeling abilities (Spearman rho up to 0.80), while structure-sensitive metrics, such as those measuring digit and line-break handling, correlate with task accuracy. We hope TokEval enables more principled tokenizer evaluation, replacing pretraining sweeps with intrinsic measurement wherever the two agree.

</details>


### [138] [Multi-Agent AI System for Radiology Report Structuring and Quality Assurance with Independent Radiologist Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18072)
*Iryna Hartsock,Cesar Lam,Christopher Otteni,Aliya Qayyum,Robert Gatenby,Cyrillo Araujo,Ghulam Rasool*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文开发并评估了一个本地部署的多智能体AI系统，用于放射学报告结构化与质量控制。系统成功将638份CT报告的22270个句子结构化到标准解剖学格式，并标记出14.1%的报告存在质量问题。两名放射科医生评估显示69%的报告重构正确，未遗漏重要临床信息或引入伪造内容，84%的报告整体QA表现被评为"优秀"或"良好"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 放射学报告格式不统一、质量参差不齐，需要自动化的报告结构化与质量控制工具，以提升报告的标准化程度和临床质量。

Method: 采用多智能体AI流水线，结合正则规则和本地大语言模型，对句子级别的报告进行解剖学结构标准化；同时检测发现部分与印象部分的不匹配、性别与解剖结构冲突以及关键发现未记录等问题。由两名委员会认证的放射科医生对45份报告子集进行独立评估。

Result: 系统成功结构化全部报告的22270个句子，标记90份（14.1%）报告存在质量问题，最常见的是分节不匹配（80份，12.5%）。放射科医生评估中，69%的报告重构正确，4%错误，27%存在分歧；一致认为无临床重要信息遗漏且无虚构内容，84%报告QA表现评为"优秀"或"良好"。

Conclusion: 本地部署的多智能体AI系统能在单一工作流中结合报告结构化与质量控制功能，在放射科医生评估中表现良好，可能有助于推动放射学报告标准化和质量保证。

Abstract: Purpose: To develop and evaluate a locally deployed multi-agent AI system for radiology report structuring and quality assurance. Materials and Methods: This retrospective study included 638 radiology reports from CT examinations of the chest, abdomen, and pelvis dictated by 15 board-certified radiologists in 2023 and 2024. A multi-agent AI pipeline was developed to perform report structuring and quality assurance (QA). The system structured the report into standardized anatomical sections at the sentence level using regex rules and local large language models. It also detected mismatches between the Findings and Impression sections, or within sections; gender-anatomy conflicts; and undocumented communication of critical findings. Two board-certified radiologists independently evaluated a 45-report subset. Results: The multi-agent system structured the Findings sections of all reports (22,270 sentences) into a predefined anatomical format while retaining the original report content. The system flagged 90 (14.1%) reports, most commonly for section mismatches (80 reports, 12.5%). In the radiologist evaluation, both reviewers agreed that 31 (69%) were correctly restructured, 2 reports (4%) were incorrectly restructured, and disagreed on the remaining 12 reports (27%). Both reviewers agreed that no clinically important information was omitted and no fabricated content was introduced. Overall QA performance was rated as "excellent" or "good" in 84% of the evaluated reports, with the remaining reports rated as "fair". Conclusion: A locally deployed multi-agent AI system combined radiology report structuring and quality assurance within a single workflow. The system demonstrated favorable performance in radiologist evaluation. Such systems may support standardization of reporting and quality assurance in radiology practice.

</details>

### [139] [Entropic Value-at-Risk portfolio optimization for tempered stable Lévy processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18022)
*Jaehyung Choi*

Main category: q-fin.PM

TL;DR: 本文提出了针对 tempered stable Lévy 收益分布的参数化熵风险价值（EVaR）投资组合优化方法，通过两种多元构造推导出组合累积生成函数和可接受的矩生成函数域，实现无需重复组合级分布拟合即可评估组合EVaR，并在2000至2026年美国行业ETF滚动样本外测试中验证了其优于CVaR组合和标准基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的CVaR投资组合优化在处理具有厚尾和非对称特征的金融收益时存在局限，而EVaR作为一种更精确的风险度量具有理论优势，但缺乏针对tempered stable Lévy过程的参数化实现方法。

Method: 采用两种多元构造——多元正态tempered stable方法和独立成分分解——推导出组合的累积生成函数及依赖于权重的矩生成函数可行域，从而可以直接从已拟合的资产级或成分级参数评估组合EVaR，无需重复进行组合级分布拟合。

Result: 在2000至2026年美国行业ETF的滚动样本外测试中，多个熵型投资组合实现了比匹配的CVaR组合和标准配置基准更高的实际夏普比率。

Conclusion: 基于tempered stable Lévy收益的参数化EVaR投资组合优化方法在实证中展现出优于CVaR组合和基准的样本外表现，验证了熵风险度量在投资组合优化中的有效性。

Abstract: We develop parametric Entropic Value-at-Risk (EVaR) portfolio optimization for tempered stable Lévy returns. We derive portfolio cumulant-generating functions and weight-dependent admissible moment-generating-function domains under two multivariate constructions: a multivariate normal tempered stable approach and an independent component factorization. These expressions allow portfolio EVaR to be evaluated from fitted asset- or component-level parameters without repeated portfolio-level distribution fitting. We construct minimum-EVaR portfolios and two entropic reward--risk portfolios. We test the portfolios in a rolling 2000 to 2026 out-of-sample U.S. sector ETF allocation. In this universe, several entropic portfolios have higher realized Sharpe ratios than their matched CVaR portfolios or standard allocation benchmarks.

</details>


<div id='cs.MM'></div>

# cs.MM [[Back]](#toc)

### [140] [On computational approaches to Pop music culture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17812)
*Arthur Flexer*

Main category: cs.MM

TL;DR: 本文主张流行音乐文化研究需要超越单纯的音频分析，采用多模态方式（音频、视觉、文本、文化）进行大规模计算分析，并回顾了现有工作，指出多模态方法稀缺和语料库采样外部效度不足两大问题，最后提出三个未来研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流行音乐文化是包含音频、视觉、文本和文化内涵的丰富综合体，现有计算研究大多局限于音频分析，缺乏集成式的多模态视角，需要借鉴数字人文中"远距离阅读"的思想，提出"远距离聆听/观看/阅读"的研究范式。

Method: 本文是一篇综述性文章，通过回顾已有的量化流行音乐研究工作，系统梳理了音频分析、视觉分析、文本分析及多模态组合分析的研究现状，并基于现有不足提出三个示例性研究框架：歌词主题图谱、专辑封面图像志、音乐时间线上的复古周期追踪。

Result: 综述发现两大核心问题：一是真正意义上的多模态研究稀缺，现有工作多为单一模态；二是音乐语料库构建中的采样实践导致外部效度存疑，影响结论的可推广性。

Conclusion: 流行音乐文化研究应向多模态、大规模的计算分析范式转型，未来应重点探索歌词主题空间、专辑封面视觉符号以及音乐风格的循环演变等方向，以更全面地理解流行音乐作为社会文化现象的本质。

Abstract: This overview article presents arguments why the computational study of Pop music culture needs to be conducted in a multi-modal way beyond mere audio analysis, gives a survey of already published quantitative work on analyzing Pop music at scale, and discusses challenges and promising research avenues for future work.
  We argue that Pop music culture is a rich tapestry of audio, visual, textual and cultural connotations and relations which needs to be studied in an integrative way as a multi-modal socio-cultural phenomenon. What is needed is an approach which is reminiscent of "distant reading", i.e. algorithmic analysis of thousands of books as a research tool in digital humanities. In addition to listening to audio, algorithms need to view album artwork and music videos, to read meta-information, lyrics, music magazines and books.
  Our review of already available work on distant reading/listening/viewing and multi-modal combinations thereof reveals two major open issues: a scarcity of truly multi-modal approaches and questionable external validity rooted in sampling practices when building music corpora. In trying to overcome these shortcomings we sketch three exemplary avenues for future research on Pop music culture: charting the topic universe of music lyrics, providing an iconography of album cover art, tracking retro cycles in music's timeline.

</details>


<div id='eess.IV'></div>

### [141] [ORViT-DR: Ordinally-Robust Hybrid ViT for Low-Resolution Diabetic Retinopathy Grading](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16958)
*Soumit Kumar Kundu,Nabil Ashab,Bidhan Biswas,Shahadat Hossain Sohag,Saif Mahmud Parvez,Souvik Kumar Kundu,Zunayed Ahmed Rafi*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文提出了ORViT-DR，一种结合CNN与Transformer的混合深度学习框架，用于从低分辨率视网膜图像中进行糖尿病视网膜病变分级。在RetinaMNIST数据集上实现了57.00%的分类准确率、0.5963的二次加权kappa值和0.4293的宏F1分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 糖尿病视网膜病变是视力受损的主要原因之一，需要一个可靠且准确的自动化分级系统。由于DR病程渐进发展，疾病严重程度分级具有天然的有序结构（相邻类别共享相似的视觉特征），现有的方法在此任务上仍有改进空间。

Method: 该方法采用混合深度学习框架ORViT-DR，核心是预训练的ViT-Hybrid骨干网络，整合了BiT-ResNetv2与Vision Transformer架构，将卷积特征提取与基于Transformer的全局上下文建模相结合。训练策略包括渐进式层解冻、层间学习率衰减、指数移动平均（EMA）参数更新以及推理阶段的集成预测。

Result: 在RetinaMNIST官方测试集上，所提方法实现了57.00%的分类准确率、0.5963的二次加权kappa分数和0.4293的宏F1分数。

Conclusion: 实验结果表明，混合CNN-Transformer架构能够为有序视网膜图像分析提供有效的特征表示，为DR自动分级提供了有前景的方案。

Abstract: Diabetic retinopathy (DR) is one of the main causes of impaired vision. A good and reliable automated grading system can make the screening process safer and more accurate. Because DR stages progress gradually, the task of grading disease severity naturally follows an ordinal structure in which neighboring classes share similar visual characteristics. In this study, ORViT-DR, a hybrid deep learning framework, is designed to improve DR grading from low-resolution retinal images. The proposed approach combines convolutional feature extraction with transformer-based global context modeling through a pre-trained ViT-Hybrid backbone, which integrates BiT-ResNetv2 with a Vision Transformer architecture. The approach is tested on the RetinaMNIST subset of the MedMNISTv2 dataset, which contains 28x28 retinal fundus images annotated with five levels of disease severity. To promote stable training and better feature learning, the training strategy applies progressive layer unfreezing, layer-wise learning rate decay, exponential moving average (EMA) parameter updates, and ensemble-based prediction during inference. Experimental results on the official RetinaMNIST test set show that the proposed method achieves 57.00% classification accuracy, along with a quadratic weighted kappa score of 0.5963 and a macro-F1 score of 0.4293. These results suggest that hybrid CNN-Transformer architectures can provide effective representations for ordinal retinal image analysis.

</details>


### [142] [MagViT: Interpretable Multi-Magnification Transformers with Patient-Level Model Selection for Breast Histopathology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16959)
*Nabil Ashab,Soumit Kumar Kundu,Saif Mahmud Parvez,Shahadat Hossain Sohag,Bidhan Biswas,Nazmus Subha*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文提出MagViT，一个可解释的多倍率Transformer框架，用于乳腺组织病理图像分类。模型使用ViT骨干提取四种倍率（40X-400X）的特征，通过可学习的尺度门控融合缺失倍率，并采用患者级五折交叉验证选择最优分支。在BreakHis数据集上取得图像准确率0.9191、患者准确率0.9643和宏F1 0.9042，并在BUSI和IDC外部数据集上验证了跨数据集泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 乳腺癌是女性最常见癌症之一，早期检测对治疗至关重要。组织病理图像分类面临两大挑战：一是需要处理多尺度组织形态信息，二是需要超越源域的临床泛化能力。现有方法在这两方面均有不足，因此需要一种能同时处理多倍率特征并具备跨数据集鲁棒性的可解释模型。

Method: MagViT框架包括：1）使用ViT骨干网络分别提取四种BreakHis倍率（40X、100X、200X、400X）的每尺度表示；2）通过可学习的门控机制融合多尺度特征，并支持屏蔽缺失倍率；3）采用患者级五折交叉验证（固定种子）运行，与三个架构分支对比，选择患者级准确率最高且融合路径最简单的分支作为最终模型；4）使用Grad-CAM进行可解释性可视化。

Result: 在BreakHis上，模型达到平均图像准确率0.9191、平均患者准确率0.9643、平均宏F1 0.9042。外部迁移实验中，在BUSI上取得图像准确率0.8306、宏F1 0.7480、患者准确率0.8291；在IDC上取得图像准确率0.8577、宏F1 0.8191、患者准确率0.8372。Grad-CAM可视化显示模型关注了不同倍率下具有诊断意义的区域。

Conclusion: MagViT通过尺度门控融合和患者级模型选择策略，在乳腺组织病理多倍率分类任务中取得了优异的性能。实验表明该方法不仅在本数据集上表现良好，还在受控适应条件下展现了跨数据集泛化潜力，且具备良好的可解释性。研究强调了患者级选择策略和可复现协议对ViT类模型在BreakHis任务中的重要性。

Abstract: Breast cancer is one of the most common types of cancer among women around the world. Rapid detection and early treatment can hinder its progress to more complex stages and can impede its spread to other parts of the body. Histopathological image classification is the most common task in cancer detection due to its robustness in analyzing cellular data. Breast histopathology classification requires handling both multi-scale tissue morphology and clinically relevant generalization beyond the source domain. This paper presents MagViT, an interpretable multi-magnification transformer framework with scale-gated fusion and patient-level model selection. The model uses four BreakHis magnifications (40X, 100X, 200X, 400X) and extracts per-scale representations with a ViT backbone, and combines them via a learnable gate that masks missing scales. Patient-level five-fold cross-validation with a fixed seed has been run and compared with three architectural branches. The most accurate branch is then selected as the final model due to the strongest patient-level accuracy while retaining the simplest fusion pathway. On BreakHis, our architecture achieves a mean image accuracy of 0.9191, a mean patient accuracy of 0.9643, and a mean macro-F1 of 0.9042. External transfer experiments provide preliminary evidence of cross-dataset generalization under controlled adaptation settings on BUSI (image accuracy 0.8306, macro-F1 0.7480, patient accuracy 0.8291) and IDC (image accuracy 0.8577, macro-F1 0.8191, patient accuracy 0.8372). Grad-CAM visualization indicates that the model focuses on diagnostically significant and meaningful regions across magnifications. Relative to prior ViT-centered BreakHis work, this study emphasizes patient-level selection and cross-dataset robustness under a reproducible protocol.

</details>


### [143] [Clinical Pathways Matter for Multimodal Deep Learning in Early Alzheimers Disease Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16962)
*Yao Lu,Solveig Kristina Hammonds,Alvaro Fernandez-Quilez*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于SigLIP的零样本多模态框架，将结构MRI嵌入与常规临床变量的文本嵌入相结合，用于阿尔茨海默病早期风险分层。在ADNI队列416名受试者中，单次就诊设置下结合MRI、MMSE、年龄和性别达到AUC 0.91±0.02，优于基于CSF Aβ42和基于MMSE的基线模型，且无需任务特定微调即可扩展到纵向数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 阿尔茨海默病早期（临床前及轻度认知障碍阶段）的风险识别仍具挑战性。现有深度学习方法虽以结构MRI作为非侵入性生物标志物有前景，但多模态模型需要任务特定训练，且依赖临床实践中不常规可用的生物标志物，缺乏实用性和可扩展性。

Method: 基于SigLIP的零样本多模态框架：1）使用未经微调的SigLIP提取MRI嵌入和临床文本嵌入；2）将两种嵌入组合为多模态表示；3）对ADNI队列中416名个体进行4年内AD风险预测；4）比较单次就诊与两次就诊设置下的性能，评估纵向信息价值和框架可扩展性。

Result: 单次就诊设置中，结合MRI嵌入与MMSE、年龄、性别的模型AUC达0.91±0.02，优于CSF Aβ42模型（AUC 0.73±0.08）和MMSE模型（AUC 0.85±0.22）。两次就诊设置性能保持或提升，支持扩展到纵向数据。

Conclusion: 零样本多模态融合结构MRI与常规临床变量，可能为早期AD风险分层提供一种实用且可扩展的策略，无需任务特定重新训练即可应用于临床实践。

Abstract: Identifying individuals at risk of Alzheimer's disease (AD), particularly in the preclinical and early stages, remains challenging. Although deep learning approaches based on structural MRI show promise as a non-invasive biomarker, existing multimodal models require task-specific training and depend on biomarkers that are not routinely available in clinical practice. Here, we propose a zero-shot multimodal framework based on SigLIP that combines structural MRI embeddings with text embeddings of routinely collected clinical variables for early AD risk stratification in individuals at preclinical or mild cognitive impairment (MCI) stages. We evaluated the approach in 416 individuals from the ADNI cohort (age: 72.73 +- 6.7). SigLIP was used without fine-tuning to extract MRI and clinical text embeddings, which were combined into multimodal representations for individual-level AD risk prediction within 4 years. We further compared the model performance in a single-visit and two-visit settings to assess the value of longitudinal information and framework scalability. In the single-visit setting, combining MRI embeddings with MMSE, age, and sex achieved an AUC of 0.91 +- 0.02, outperforming both a CSF A\b{eta}42-based model (AUC 0.73 +- 0.08) and an MMSE-based model (AUC 0.85 +- 0.22). In the two-visit setting, performance was maintained or improved, supporting the scalability of the approach to longitudinal data. These findings suggest that zero-shot multimodal fusion of structural MRI and routinely collected clinical variables may provide a practical and scalable strategy for early AD risk stratification without task-specific retraining.

</details>


### [144] [Technically Plausible but Clinically Misleading? Expert Evaluation of Patient-Personalized Synthetic Prostate MRI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16964)
*Gabriel Paulo Maglalang Israel,Sol Gedde,Alvaro Fernandez-Quilez*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于3D扩散模型的个性化前列腺MRI合成技术，该模型以患者年龄和PSA等常规临床变量为条件生成图像。虽然技术上合成图像与真实MRI相当，但放射科医生的专家评审显示，个性化合成图像在临床可信度和诊断信心方面存在变异性，并可能引入误导性线索，因此需要谨慎评估后才能安全地整合到临床工作流程中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MRI是前列腺癌评估的核心手段，但采集成本高且耗时，成为患者诊疗路径中的主要瓶颈。个性化MRI合成有望减少对扫描仪采集图像的依赖，为每个患者生成临床逼真的图像，但其临床可行性和对专家解读的影响尚未得到充分研究。

Method: 使用3D扩散模型，以年龄和PSA等常规临床变量为条件生成个性化前列腺MRI；在大型公开数据集上使用标准图像相似度指标验证合成图像的技术质量；随后进行试点单盲随机专家研究，由放射科医生使用配对评估和李克特量表对个性化与无条件合成MRI的临床可信度、信心和误导风险进行评分，并辅以定性反馈。

Result: 个性化合成MRI在技术上具有可行性且与扫描获取的MRI相当，但专家解读显示，当常规临床数据嵌入图像生成时，医生的诊断信心存在变异性，且存在产生误导性线索的潜在风险。

Conclusion: 虽然个性化MRI合成在技术上可行，但在安全整合到临床工作流程之前，需要仔细评估生成线索对临床医生解读的影响。

Abstract: Magnetic resonance imaging (MRI) is central to prostate cancer assessment, yet its acquisition is costly and time-consuming, making it a major bottleneck in the patient care pathway. Personalized MRI synthesis is a promising direction because it may allow the generation of clinically realistic images tailored to individual patients while reducing the dependence on scanner-acquired imaging. In this work, we in-vestigate whether patient-personalized MRI synthesis produces images that experts perceive as clinically plausible and how such personaliza-tion influences expert interpretation. We synthesize patient-personalized prostate MRI using a 3D diffusion model conditioned on routine pre-imaging clinical variables age and PSA. Using a large publicly available dataset, we first verify that synthesized images are technically compara-ble to scanner-acquired MRIs using standard image similarity metrics. We then conduct a pilot, single-blinded, randomized expert study on a subset of cases sampled to reflect variation in patient clinical pro-files. A radiologist compared personalized and unconditioned synthetic MRIs using paired assessments and Likert-scale ratings of clinical plausibility, confidence, and risk of misleading interpretation, supplemented by qualitative feedback. Although personalized synthetic MRIs appeared technically plausible, expert interpretation highlighted variability in con-fidence and potential risks of misleading cues when routine clinical data was embedded into image generation. These findings suggest that while personalized synthesis may be technically feasible, careful assessment is needed to understand how generated cues influence clinician interpreta-tion before such systems can be safely integrated into clinical workflows.

</details>


### [145] [Harnessing Magnitude-Only and Complex Measurements for Improved Dynamic MRI Reconstruction with Learned Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18036)
*Mahdi Saberi,Yaşar Utku Alçalar,Merve Gülle,Chetan Shenoy,Mehmet Akçakaya*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文提出C+Mag方法，利用辅助k空间幅度信息加速动态MRI重建，通过ADMM展开框架和幅度感知数据保真度公式，在回顾性和前瞻性欠采样数据上均优于传统PD-DL方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MRI重建自然利用复值k空间数据，而稀疏相位恢复研究表明幅度-only测量能提供互补信息。然而幅度信息在MRI重建中的应用尚未被充分探索，主要原因是缺乏不增加扫描时间即可获取有信息量幅度测量的实际场景。

Method: 提出C+Mag方法，一种幅度信息增强的物理驱动深度学习重建方法。其核心包括：(1) ADMM-based展开框架；(2) 新颖的幅度感知数据保真度公式；(3) 引入二次平滑优化和动量更新来处理幅度约束的不可微性和非凸性。利用了稳态动态MRI中k空间幅度跨时间帧强一致性的观察。

Result: 在回顾性欠采样电影MRI和相位对比流动MRI数据集，以及前瞻性欠采样实时电影MRI采集中，该方法相比传统PD-DL方法展现出更好的伪影抑制、更锐利的解剖结构恢复和更好的相位信息保留，并经盲法专家评估进一步验证。

Conclusion: k空间幅度信息可以作为动态MRI重建的有效互补信号，C+Mag方法通过巧妙的数学处理克服了幅度约束带来的优化挑战，在加速稳态动态MRI重建中展现出显著优势。

Abstract: MRI reconstruction methods for undersampled k-space data naturally utilize complex-valued measurements. Parallel developments in sparse phase retrieval have shown that magnitude-only measurements may provide complementary information for signal recovery. However, their use in MRI reconstruction remains largely unexplored, due to lack of practical settings where informative magnitude measurements can be obtained without additional scan time. In this work, we investigate the use of auxiliary k-space magnitude information for accelerated steady-state dynamic MRI reconstruction, and demonstrate strong consistency of k-space magnitudes across time-frames. Building on this observation, we propose $\mathbb{C}+\text{Mag}$, a magnitude-informed physics-driven deep learning reconstruction method. The proposed method employs an ADMM-based unrolling framework with a novel magnitude-aware data-fidelity formulation, where quadratically smoothed optimization and momentum-based updates are introduced to address the non-differentiability and non-convexity of the magnitude constraints. Experiments on retrospectively undersampled cine MRI and phase-contrast flow MRI datasets, as well as prospectively undersampled real-time cine MRI acquisitions, demonstrate improved artifact suppression, sharper anatomical recovery, and better preservation of phase information compared to conventional PD-DL methods, which is further supported through blinded expert reader evaluations.

</details>


### [146] [Primitive Representation Learning for Unsupervised Dynamic Contrast Enhanced MRI Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18055)
*Veronika Spieker,Wenqi Huang,Cemre Ariyurek,Liam Timms,Daniel Rueckert,Onur Afacan,Julia A. Schnabel,Sila Kurugol*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 提出一种基于原型函数的多维动态对比增强MRI重建框架，将解剖结构、动态对比增强和残余运动分解为独立的时间基函数，在无大数据训练的情况下实现与常规方法相当的重建质量和增强曲线提取精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态对比增强MRI需要高倍欠采样下的高质量时空重建，现有高斯和高斯原型函数方法虽无需大规模训练数据，但未考虑动态对比这一额外维度。

Method: 提出多维度、基于原型函数的动态对比增强MRI重建框架，将潜在解剖结构、动态对比增强和残余运动分离为独立的时间基函数，从而实现对表示的几何解释；采用模块化分层设计以扩展至更多动态因素和更高加速率。

Result: 该架构在重建质量和主动脉及肾脏增强曲线的提取精度方面，均能达到与传统重建方法相当的性能。

Conclusion: 该多维原型函数框架为动态对比增强MRI提供了一种无需大规模训练数据的有效重建方案，其模块化设计具有良好的可扩展性。

Abstract: Reliable quantitative analysis of dynamic contrast-enhanced MRI requires high-quality spatiotemporal reconstructions at high undersampling rates. Scan-specific reconstructions using Gaussian and Gabor primitives have shown promising results without the need for large training datasets, but have not addressed the additional dimension of dynamic contrast. We propose a multi-dimensional, primitive based framework for dynamic contrast-enhanced MRI reconstruction that disentangles the underlying anatomy, the dynamic contrast enhancement, and residual motion into separate temporal basis functions, thereby enabling a geometrical interpretation of the representation. We show that this architecture achieves performance competitive with conventional reconstruction methods, both in reconstruction quality and in the accuracy of extracted aorta and kidney enhancement curves. The modular tier design extends naturally to additional dynamic factors and higher acceleration rates. Code available at https://github.com/compai-lab/ 2026-GaborDCE-spieker.

</details>


<div id='q-fin.ST'></div>

### [147] [Potential of ChatGPT in predicting stock market trends based on Twitter Sentiment Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2311.06273)
*Ummara Mumtaz,Summaya Mumtaz*

Main category: q-fin.ST

TL;DR: 本研究探讨ChatGPT是否能够仅通过社交媒体推文和情感分析来预测股票市场走势，重点关注微软和谷歌两家科技巨头。研究发现ChatGPT的评估与次日股票表现之间存在正相关性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ChatGPT的出现给AI领域带来了重大转变，其在对话和语言理解方面表现出色。研究者希望探索ChatGPT是否能够利用Twitter等平台的海量情感数据来提供股票趋势预测，以拓展其应用价值。

Method: 研究通过分析社交媒体推文的情感（正面、负面或中性）来判断其对微软和谷歌两家科技公司股价的影响，利用ChatGPT进行情感分析和市场预测。

Result: 研究发现ChatGPT的评估与两家科技公司次日的股票表现之间存在正相关关系，表明ChatGPT的情感分析能够为股票预测提供有价值的参考。

Conclusion: 该研究丰富了我们对ChatGPT适应性的认识，并强调了AI在金融市场监管预测中日益增长的重要性。

Abstract: The rise of ChatGPT has brought a notable shift to the AI sector, with its exceptional conversational skills and deep grasp of language. Recognizing its value across different areas, our study investigates ChatGPT's capacity to predict stock market movements using only social media tweets and sentiment analysis. We aim to see if ChatGPT can tap into the vast sentiment data on platforms like Twitter to offer insightful predictions about stock trends. We focus on determining if a tweet has a positive, negative, or neutral effect on two big tech giants Microsoft and Google's stock value. Our findings highlight a positive link between ChatGPT's evaluations and the following days stock results for both tech companies. This research enriches our view on ChatGPT's adaptability and emphasizes the growing importance of AI in shaping financial market forecasts.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [148] [GxP-Agent: Process-DAG Topology for Reliable Clinical Trial Programming with LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16890)
*Jaime Yan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出GxP-Agent多智能体系统，将临床实验编程过程编码为有向无环图（DAG），将数据集生成分解为15个领域特定节点，在CDISC-Bench基准上实现100%结构匹配，远超所有基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床实验编程（将研究方案转化为符合CDISC标准的分析数据集）是监管申报中的瓶颈，而基于LLM的代码生成在该任务上完全失败——5个前沿模型11次单次尝试均无法生成有效的受试者级分析数据集。

Method: 提出GxP-Agent多智能体系统：将监管流程顺序编码为DAG，将单体数据集生成分解为15个领域特定节点，由具备pharmaverse技能上下文的worker agent执行，并配备验证门控和条件重试机制。同时构建了基于FDA试点提交CDISCPilot01的执行型基准CDISC-Bench。

Result: GxP-Agent配Claude Sonnet 4.6在CDISC-Bench上实现100%结构匹配（49/49变量，254条记录，三次独立运行），远超最佳检索增强基线59.2%；弱模型GPT-4.1在DAG架构下从0%提升至59.2%；在ADAE数据集（9节点分支DAG，55变量，1191条记录）上首次尝试即达100%结构匹配。

Conclusion: 将领域流程知识编码为图拓扑结构而非依赖LLM推理，是实现可靠的、符合GxP规范临床实验编程的关键使能因素。

Abstract: Clinical trial programming -- transforming study protocols into analysis-ready datasets under CDISC standards -- is a bottleneck in regulatory submissions, yet LLM-based code generation fails catastrophically on this task: across 11 single-shot attempts with five frontier models, none produces a valid subject-level analysis dataset. We introduce GxP-Agent, a multi-agent system that encodes regulatory process ordering as a directed acyclic graph (DAG), decomposing monolithic dataset generation into 15 domain-specific nodes executed by worker agents with pharmaverse skill context, validation gates, and conditional retry. On CDISC-Bench, a new execution-based benchmark built from the FDA pilot submission CDISCPilot01 (254 subjects, 49 ground-truth ADSL variables), GxP-Agent with Claude Sonnet 4.6 achieves 100% structural match (49/49 variables, 254 correct records) across three independent runs, compared to 59.2% for the best retrieval-augmented baseline and 0% for all single-agent and flat multi-agent approaches. The DAG topology also enables weaker models: GPT-4.1 achieves 59.2% mean structural match under the same DAG, where it scores 0% under every other architecture. The approach generalizes to ADAE (adverse events; 9-node branching DAG, 55 variables, 1,191 records), achieving 100% structural match on the first attempt. These results demonstrate that encoding domain process knowledge as graph topology -- rather than relying on LLM reasoning alone -- is a key enabler for reliable, GxP-compliant clinical trial programming.

</details>


### [149] [Runtime Governance for Agentic AI: Action-Boundary Control with Trusted Provenance and Fail-Closed Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16891)
*Adam Mazzocchetti*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍Aegis，一个面向Agentic AI系统的运行时治理体系。Aegis将模型输出视为动作提案，通过可信决策层在工具执行前进行调解，实现"模型提议、可信运行时决策"的架构。在6300行实验数据中，Aegis实现了零治理级风险副作用，并完整保留了溯源与仲裁证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Agentic AI系统可执行修改文件、发送消息、启动任务等操作，安全风险从有害文本生成转向有害操作副作用。仅靠提示层面治理无法建立执行边界，需要运行时治理系统来防止模型生成的危险操作真正落地执行。

Method: Aegis采用"动作提案-可信运行时决策"模式：模型输出被视为提案，由可信决策层在工具执行前进行评估；依据活跃策略状态评估提案、在服务端解析溯源、在不确定时默认关闭（fail closed），并针对选定案例通过基于法定人数的Senate式结算（quorum-based non-unilateral authorization）进行授权。

Result: 在五个运行家族、42个任务、三种条件、每家族10次重复的沙箱语料上：6300行数据中提示策略条件化产生79行风险比较器路径泄漏；2100行Aegis治理行中录得零治理级mock工具应用和零治理级风险副作用完成；1832行Aegis尝试治理行全部保留可信溯源；1019行Senate结算行全部具备法定人数和最终签名计数证据。

Conclusion: 实验结果虽不证明通用自主Agent安全性，但在该评估沙箱语料中支持更窄的系统性声明：运行时动作边界治理成功阻止了观察到的风险提案转化为治理级副作用。

Abstract: Agentic AI systems request tool actions that can modify files, send messages, launch jobs, or change workflow state. This shifts the safety problem from harmful text generation to harmful operational side effects. Prompt-level governance can shape model behavior, but it does not create an execution boundary. We introduce Aegis, a runtime governance system that treats model outputs as action proposals and mediates them through a trusted decision layer before tool execution. The model proposes; the trusted runtime decides. Aegis evaluates proposals against active policy state, resolves provenance server-side, fails closed under uncertainty, and routes selected cases through Senate-style settlement, a quorum- based non-unilateral authorization path. We evaluate Aegis on a repeated sandbox corpus spanning five run families, 42 tasks, three conditions, and ten repeats per family. Across 6,300 rows, prompt-policy conditioning produced 79 risky comparator-path leakage rows. Across 2,100 Aegis-governed rows, the system recorded zero governed mock-tool applications and zero governed risky side-effect completions. All 1,832 Aegis-attempted governed rows preserved trusted Aegis-resolved provenance, and all 1,019 Senate-settled rows had quorum and final signed tally evidence. These results do not prove general autonomous-agent safety. They support the narrower systems claim that, in this evaluated sandbox corpus, runtime action-boundary governance prevented observed risky proposals from becoming governed side effects.

</details>


### [150] [The Price of Thinking: Reasoning Effort as a Model-Specific API Contract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16956)
*Yeabin Moon*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究通过30道AIME 2026题目、每题5次调用的配对对比实验，考察API合同中"推理努力"条款（显式高努力 vs 省略）对成本与准确率的影响。结果显示显式高努力合约每次调用成本高出0.01031美元（显著），但准确率差异不显著（+0.0133，区间无法排除最高4.67个百分点的增益）。每正确回答成本在高努力合约下更高（0.08665 vs 0.07662美元）。研究还通过契约普查和原始响应探针记录了模型特定的省略语义，所有流程在结果显示前冻结，结论仅限于所研究的模型、任务和日期。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: API购买者购买的是一份标注日期的契约而非单一模型名称，其中"推理努力"条款（reasoning-effort term）是一个关键的合同要素，但其实际对成本和准确率的影响缺乏严格的实证检验。研究者希望通过受控配对对比实验来量化该条款的经济与技术后果。

Method: 采用注册配对对比设计（registered paired contrast），对比Sonnet 5在显式高努力条款与省略努力条件下（同一模型）的表现。使用30道AIME 2026数学题目，每题5次调用，每次付费尝试分配一个冻结的终末类别，推理时对题目重采样但保留重复调用。所有流程（请求注册表、解析器、终末分类法、统计方案、分析管道）在查看结果前冻结。

Result: 显式高努力合约的每次调用平均成本高出0.01031美元（95% CI: +0.00204到+0.01974），成本差异显著。准确率对比为+0.0133（95% CI: -0.0267到+0.0467），未检测到显著准确率差异，但区间允许最高4.67个百分点的增益无法排除。每正确回答成本：高努力合约0.08665美元，省略合约0.07662美元。契约普查和探针实验进一步记录了模型特定的省略语义（包括同一提供商内部），但部分原始结构不确定时仅以文档级别做出声明。

Conclusion: 在本次研究范围内，显式高推理努力条款显著增加了API调用成本，但未检测到相应的准确率提升；从成本效率角度看，省略努力条款的合约更具性价比。研究结论严格限定于所测试的模型、任务类型和数据收集日期，不能推广到其他场景。该研究展示了如何以严格预注册的方式实证评估API契约条款的实际影响。

Abstract: API buyers purchase a dated contract, not a model name alone: the contract includes the requested and served model, reasoning-effort term or its omission, output rail, service product, prompt, and price schedule. We study the reasoning-effort term through a registered paired contrast of Sonnet 5 with explicit high effort against the same model with effort omitted, using 30 AIME 2026 items and five calls per item. Every paid attempt was assigned one frozen terminal category, and inference resampled items while retaining their repeated calls. Mean delivered cost was \$0.01031 per call higher under the explicit-high contract than under the omitted contract [+\$0.00204, +\$0.01974]. The corresponding accuracy contrast was +0.0133 [-0.0267, +0.0467]; we did not detect an accuracy difference, and the interval permits a gain of up to 4.67 percentage points that this design cannot rule out. Cost per correct answer was \$0.08665 under the high-effort contract and \$0.07662 under the omitted contract, as registered point estimates. A dated contract census, Models-API metadata, and preregistered raw-response probes further documented model-specific omission semantics, including within a provider; claims remained at documentation grade when raw structure was indeterminate. The request registry, parser, terminal taxonomy, statistical plan, and analysis pipeline were frozen before outcomes were examined; the resulting claims are bounded to the model, task, and collection date studied.

</details>


### [151] [FedPref: Federated Preference Learning for Structured Radiology Report Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16971)
*Flint Xiaofeng Fan,Cheston Tan,Yew-Soon Ong,Roger Wattenhofer*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出FedPref联邦学习方法，利用冻结的公共语言模型生成候选JSON提取结果，本地标注进行排序，各站点仅共享模型更新来协同训练紧凑的Qwen3-8B适配器，实现放射学报告结构化信息提取，无需共享报告或标注数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 放射学报告以自由文本描述发现和位置，但下游检索与分析需要固定模式的关系提取。不同医疗机构的标注数据分布不均，小型医院本地证据少，直接汇总数据可能不可行，因此需要在不共享原始数据的情况下实现协作学习。

Method: FedPref方法包含三个核心环节：(1) 冻结的公共语言模型作为异构教师池，为报告生成多种备选JSON提取结果；(2) 本地标注对候选结果进行排序打分；(3) 各站点仅共享模型更新，协作训练紧凑的Qwen3-8B适配器，实现跨模型对比学习，避免单模型重复采样导致的模式坍塌。

Result: 在六个模拟医院的开发集上（数据量和疾病流行率不均），FedPref相比各站点独立训练，客户端平均F1提升2.49分，最差站点F1提升9.10分，数据量最小的站点获益最大。在400份人工验证的锁定金标准测试集上，FedPref达到68.68 F1，集中式训练为71.67 F1，保持了相同的性能排序。

Conclusion: FedPref使数据量不均且无法合并的机构能够通过协作受益于联邦学习，而无需共享报告或标注数据，特别对数据稀缺的小型医院效果显著。

Abstract: Radiology reports describe findings and locations in free text, but downstream search and analysis require these relations in a fixed schema. Learning this extraction requires labels that are unevenly distributed across institutions: smaller hospitals have less local evidence, and pooling data may be infeasible. We introduce FedPref: frozen public language models propose alternative JSON extractions, local annotations rank them, and sites collaboratively train compact Qwen3-8B adapters while sharing only model updates. A heterogeneous teacher pool provides cross-model contrast when repeated single-model samples collapse. On development data from six simulated hospitals with unequal data volume and disease prevalence, FedPref improves client-mean F1 by 2.49 points and worst-site F1 by 9.10 points compared with training each site in isolation, with the largest gains at the sites holding the least data. Central training on the pooled preference-pair union is 2.66 points higher on client-mean F1. On a locked, 400-report manually validated gold test set, FedPref reaches 68.68 F1 and pooled training 71.67, preserving that same ordering. FedPref thus lets institutions with unequal, unpooled data benefit from collaboration without ever sharing reports or annotations.

</details>


### [152] [The Problem Is the Problem: Towards Scalable Mathematical Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16977)
*Zeyu Zheng,Shengtong Zhang,Jeremy Avigad,Prasad Tetali,Sean Welleck*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的人机协作数学发现范式FAR（Find, Attempt, and Recommend），将人类输入从预先选定的单一问题转变为研究方向，系统自动在文献语料库中搜索候选问题并通过级联过滤的方式筛选，从而解决AI辅助数学发现中人类筛选问题和审阅结果这两个瓶颈阶段。在组合学试点中，从5,245篇论文中恢复6,453个候选猜想/开放问题，过滤至4,717个依然开放的问题，经推理和自动分诊后选出598个潜在解决方案和77项供团队审阅，发现了多个有价值的数学成果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI辅助数学研究的流程中，人类精力集中在前期选题和后期审阅，这两个阶段正成为研究级数学的瓶颈。由于前沿模型推理和专家评审都是稀缺资源，需要更高效的资源分配方式来提升AI辅助数学发现的效率。

Method: 提出FAR（Find, Attempt, and Recommend）级联框架，从文献到审阅的流水线：首先从大范围文献语料库中检索研究方向相关的候选问题，然后依次通过问题规范性过滤、开放性判断、推理尝试和自动化分诊等多级过滤，最终将少数高价值结果呈现给专家团队审阅。

Result: 在组合学试点中，从5,245篇论文中恢复6,453个候选猜想或开放问题，过滤后保留4,717个"无争议且仍开放"的猜想，推理和自动分诊后选出598个潜在解决结果，最终77项进入作者团队审阅，其中发现了对Davies-Jenssen-Perkins-Roberts、Erdős-Straus、Ikenmeyer-Pak-Panova和Lund-Saraf-Wolf等猜想和问题的有价值成果。

Conclusion: FAR范式证明了以研究方向而非单一问题作为人类输入、通过文献搜索与级联过滤实现AI辅助数学发现的新模式是有效的，为研究级数学中稀缺资源（人类专家和AI推理）的高效分配提供了可行方案。

Abstract: AI systems are increasingly capable of contributing to mathematical research. In research practice, frontier-model reasoning is a limited resource, and expert mathematical review is even more sharply constrained. Allocating these scarce resources well is therefore central to making AI-assisted mathematical discovery efficient. In most current AI-for-math workflows, human effort is concentrated at the beginning and end, in selecting suitable research problems and later reviewing the resulting artifacts. These two stages are becoming bottlenecks for research-level mathematics. We address them by proposing a new human-AI discovery paradigm. The human input is no longer a single problem selected in advance, but a research direction in which the experts have interest and expertise. The system then searches a broad literature corpus for candidate problems in that direction. Inspired by search and recommender systems, we build Find, Attempt, and Recommend (FAR), a literature-to-review cascade that automates the search for suitable problems and focuses human attention on artifacts that have passed several stages of filtering. In a combinatorics pilot, the pipeline starts from 5,245 combinatorics papers, recovers 6,453 candidate conjectures or open problems, and filters them to 4,717 apparently well-posed and still-open conjectures. Subsequent reasoning and automated triage stages surface 598 potential resolutions and select 77 items for author-team review. Among them, we identify many interesting discoveries, including results on conjectures and questions of Davies--Jenssen--Perkins--Roberts, Erdős--Straus, Ikenmeyer--Pak--Panova, and Lund--Saraf--Wolf. These results demonstrate the effectiveness of this new mode of human-AI collaboration for mathematical discovery.

</details>


### [153] [SkillEffect: Checked Lowering for Memory-Bounded Agent Tools](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17007)
*Yinuo Wang,Yiyu Shi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SkillEffect，一个检查化降级（checked-lowering）运行时系统，用于在执行Agent工具调用前验证和约束程序的内存边界，确保异构注册内存关系（如流式归约、有界堆Top-k）在固定的资源上限下可靠执行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型将Agent技能实例化为具体程序时，即使语义正确的程序也可能加载完整输入，超出单个工具调用的内存限制。需要在授予执行权限前进行独立验证和资源约束。

Method: SkillEffect采用检查化降级架构：每个关系插件提供源识别器、输入事实提取器、有界IR构造器、竞技场边界函数和后置条件；一个公共运行时提供检查选择、有界VM执行、原子容量租用和分阶段发布。独立的检查器根据提交的程序和不可变输入重建每个降级方案，验证后方授予执行权限。

Result: 在六个运算符家族中，有界访问显著降低峰值内存并提高固定上限下的完成率。六个插件在五种执行模式（从流式归约到有界堆Top-k）中实例化同一契约。XLSX入门研究和新关系及新保留状态模式复用了同一信任边界，检查器接受了所有合法的配置并拒绝了所有对抗性提案。

Conclusion: 一个检查化降级架构可以在Agent工具调度时强制执行异构的注册内存关系，内存约束的有效性主要来自架构设计而非自动泛化，每个支持的计算都需要审计过的关系插件。

Abstract: Agent Skills can specify procedural and resource obligations for tool use, and language models instantiate them as concrete programs. However, when models turn this guidance into code for existing tool interfaces, even a semantically correct program may load an entire input and exceed the memory available to one tool call. We present SkillEffect, a checked-lowering runtime for computations with a recoverable source relation, an audited bounded implementation, and a registered output postcondition. Before granting execution authority, an independent checker rebuilds each proposed lowering from the submitted program and immutable input. Every relation plugin supplies a source recognizer, input-fact extractor, bounded-IR constructor, arena-bound function, and postcondition; one common runtime provides checked selection, bounded-VM execution, atomic capacity leasing, and staged publication. Generality in SkillEffect is architectural rather than automatic: each supported computation requires an audited relation plugin, while the dispatch, resource-control, execution, and publication mechanisms are shared across plugins. Across six operator families, bounded access substantially reduces peak memory and improves completion under externally fixed caps. Six plugins instantiate the same contract across five execution patterns, from streaming reduction to bounded-heap Top-k. The XLSX onboarding study and Top-k extension show that a new relation and a new retained-state pattern reuse the same trust boundary, while the checker accepts all evaluated legal configurations and rejects all adversarial proposals. Together, these results show that one checked-lowering architecture can enforce heterogeneous registered memory relations at Agent tool dispatch.

</details>


### [154] [Memory Is Communication: The Frontier Between Remembering and Signaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17053)
*Yashar Talebirad,Eden Redman,Ali Parsaee,Osmar R. Zaiane*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究有限理性代理人在决策中如何分配信息预算——即同时利用自身历史和同伴信息时，如何在记忆容量与通信带宽之间取得最优权衡。作者提出"记忆-信号前沿"（remembering-signaling frontier）概念来描述达到特定任务性能阈值的资源分配可行区域，并通过参照博弈实验初步验证：当历史信息能带来更大的任务损失降低时，代理人对同伴通信的需求更小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有限理性代理人在获取决策信息时面临双重约束：自身记忆容量有限，同伴通信带宽也有限。已有研究分别探讨了记忆或通信的作用，但缺乏一个统一框架来分析两者间的联合权衡与最优分配问题。

Method: 提出了"记忆-信号前沿"的量化分析框架，用以刻画在固定任务和决策规则下，记忆速率与消息速率组合达成性能阈值时的可行区域。通过参照博弈（referential games）进行实验验证，操控记忆与消息速率来估计前沿曲线。

Result: 初步实验中，目标重复出现与更短的成功消息相关联，而来自隐藏循环规则的可预测性并未缩短消息长度，暗示并非所有历史冗余都能同样有效地减少通信需求。

Conclusion: 作者提出可检验的假设：若历史信息对任务损失的降低幅度更大，则有限代理人对同伴通信的需求更低。未来可通过控制记忆和消息速率的实验来估计前沿并验证该预测。

Abstract: A bounded agent may obtain information for a decision from its own past, from peers, or from both sources. Retaining task-relevant history can reduce later communication, while a peer message can supply what memory lacks. Under limits on both resources, how should an agent allocate its information budget? Given a fixed task and decision rule, the memory and message rate pairs attaining a performance threshold form an achievable region under specified rules for using history and peer observations. We call its efficient boundary the remembering--signaling frontier. Across conditions where history permits the same maximum reduction in task loss, we hypothesize that a bounded agent will need less peer communication when it obtains a larger loss reduction from history. In preliminary referential games, target repetition coincided with shorter successful messages, while predictability from a hidden cyclic rule did not shorten them. Experiments varying memory and message rates can estimate the frontier and test this prediction across cooperative tasks.

</details>


### [155] [DiSCO: Defending text-to-image generation through distribution-guided contrastive prompt optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17067)
*Tong Zhang,Motasem Alfarra,Carlos Hinojosa,Christos Louizos,Bernard Ghanem*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出DiSCO，一种零样本、纯黑盒的文本到图像生成模型安全防御方法，通过分布引导的后缀扩展和对比评分，在不修改模型内部的情况下有效降低NSFW内容生成率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有防御方法大多基于白盒假设，依赖文本编码器优化、权重编辑或推理时干预，无法扩展到专有模型。黑盒替代方案虽应用范围更广，但在"良性对抗"问题（语言上安全但触发有害生成）上失效。

Method: DiSCO作为纯提示层面的即插即用模块，采用基于束搜索的分布引导后缀扩展，通过对目标模型自身生成的安全/不安全图像池进行对比评分来优化，并通过迭代自适应反馈直至产生安全内容。

Result: 在I2P基准上，DiSCO对未防御和已防御模型分别实现了37.7%和25.13%的攻击成功率（ASR）降低，同时保持语义保真度并提升图像连贯性。

Conclusion: DiSCO作为黑盒、架构无关的模块，可直接应用于任何文本到图像系统，无需对模型本身做任何修改，为专有模型的NSFW内容安全防护提供了一条可行路径。

Abstract: As text-to-image generative models advance, they raise critical safety concerns, particularly the generation of Not-Safe-For-Work (NSFW) content such as violence and nudity, further exacerbated by red-teaming adversarial attacks. Existing defenses predominantly operate under white-box assumptions, relying on text encoder optimization, weight editing, or inference-time intervention, and fundamentally cannot scale to proprietary models. Black-box alternatives based on LLM prompt rewriting offer broader applicability, yet fail in a critical regime we identify as the \textit{benign adversarial} problem: prompts that are linguistically safe but still trigger harmful generation due to the model's learned data distribution. We propose DiSCO, a zero-shot, strictly black-box defense that operates entirely at the prompt level as a plug-and-play module, requiring no model retraining, fine-tuning, or access to model internals. DiSCO performs distribution-guided suffix expansion via beam search, optimized through contrastive scoring over safe and unsafe image pools generated by the target model itself, with iterative adaptive feedback until safe content is produced. We demonstrate that DiSCO consistently enhances the safety of both undefended and defended models on the I2P benchmark under multiple red-teaming attacks, achieving 37.7% and 25.13% ASR reduction, respectively, while maintaining semantic fidelity and improving image coherence. As a black-box, architecture-agnostic module, DiSCO can be readily applied to any text-to-image system without necessitating any changes to the model itself.

</details>


### [156] [KernelArc: A Multi-Agent Framework for GPU Kernel Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17071)
*Joyjit Kundu,Ben Stoffelen,Kaili Wang,Peter Vrancx,Ludovic Denoyer*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了KernelArc，一个用于异构负载下自主GPU内核优化的多智能体框架，在SOL-ExecBench基准上取得了多项第一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GPU内核优化是极其复杂的任务，单一优化策略难以覆盖异构工作负载的多样性。作者希望通过共享多智能体搜索来拓宽探索空间，在固定候选预算内找到更强的优化方案。

Method: KernelArc框架包含多个策略专门化的智能体并行运行，通过仅结论的共享内存进行协调，配备确定性基准测试守卫，以及基于只读跨智能体状态和平台期触发的草稿机制。

Result: 在NVIDIA H100和B200 GPU上评估了SOL-ExecBench的代表性工作负载，生成的内核涵盖自定义BF16 GEMM、静态cuBLASLt配置表、融合MoE反向传播、形状门控解码器层融合、NVFP4分组查询注意力和分页预填充注意力。在2026年7月30日的公共排行榜快照中，在L1、L2、量化及FlashInfer任务上均排名第一。

Conclusion: 共享多智能体搜索能有效拓宽探索范围并在固定预算内达到更强的最优结果，而各协调特征的价值取决于具体内核类型和优化阶段。

Abstract: We present KernelArc, a multi-agent framework for autonomous GPU kernel optimization across heterogeneous workloads. Strategy-specialized agents run in parallel and coordinate through conclusions-only shared memory, a deterministic benchmark guard, and read-only cross-agent state with plateau-triggered drafting. We evaluate \kernelarc{} on NVIDIA H100 and B200 GPUs using category-representative SOL-ExecBench workloads. The resulting implementations span custom BF16 GEMM, static cuBLASLt Expert-API configuration tables, fused mixture-of-experts backward, shape-gated decoder-layer fusion, native NVFP4 grouped-query attention, and paged prefill attention. At the public SOL-ExecBench leaderboard snapshot recorded on July~30, 2026, these submissions ranked first on representative L1, L2, Quantization, and FlashInfer tasks. The trajectories support the paper's central motivation: shared multi-agent search can broaden exploration and reach stronger incumbents within a fixed candidate budget, while the value of individual coordination features depends on the kernel and optimization stage.

</details>


### [157] [A decodability criterion predicts when hidden-state selection beats majority voting in large language models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17124)
*Zhixiang wang,Ziliang Hong,Ulas Bagci*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CASE（Correctness-Axis SElection），一种基于答案token隐藏态训练线性门控的候选答案选择方法，并引入"decodability"指标用于预判隐藏态选择是否优于多数投票，实验表明其在不同模型上最多提升19个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型采样答案的融合通常依赖多数投票，但投票在难题上因错误答案共享相关性而失效。基于隐藏态读取正确性信号的替代方案虽具潜力，但其准确性随模型和任务变化且缺乏可信度衡量指标。

Method: 提出CASE方法，在答案token的隐藏态上训练线性门控并对候选答案打分，选择得分最高者。同时引入decodability指标——一种无泄漏的度量，衡量门控对某问题正确与错误候选的排序能力，以此预测隐藏态选择是否优于投票。

Result: 在保留数据上，decodability预测选择相对于投票的准确率增益，Pearson相关系数r=0.75，决策阈值接近AUC=0.60。在通用和医学LLM上，CASE在中等难度问题上比投票提升最多19个百分点，在难题上提升16.8个百分点；decodability的预测在未见科学领域内转移误差仅3.8个百分点。

Conclusion: decodability依赖于模型需回忆的对齐知识而非模型规模，可提前测量，为给定模型和任务在"学习式选择"与"多数投票"之间提供实用的决策准则。

Abstract: Combining the answers a large language model (LLM) samples for a question into one decision is a test-time information fusion problem, usually solved by majority voting. Voting is unreliable on difficult questions, where the sampled answers share correlated errors, so the wrong answer can win and drawing more samples makes the decision worse. Selecting a candidate by reading a correctness signal from the model's hidden states is a promising alternative, but its accuracy varies across models and tasks, and no measure indicates when it can be trusted. In this paper, we propose CASE (Correctness-Axis SElection), a dynamic selection combiner that trains a linear gate on the answer-token hidden state and selects the highest-scoring candidate. Its main contribution is decodability, a leakage-free measure of how well the gate ranks a question's correct candidates above its incorrect ones, which predicts whether hidden-state selection will outperform voting. A conventional probe appears accurate only because of question-identity leakage, which vanishes under question-grouped evaluation. On held-out data, decodability predicts the accuracy gain of selection over voting with a Pearson correlation r=0.75 and a decision threshold near AUC=0.60. Across general and medical LLMs, CASE improves over voting by up to 19 points on medium-difficulty questions and 16.8 points on hard questions. Decodability depends on the aligned knowledge a model must recall, not on its scale, and its prediction transfers to an unseen scientific domain within 3.8 points. It thus provides a practical criterion, measurable in advance for a given model and task, for choosing between learned selection and majority voting.

</details>


### [158] [Toward Personal Intelligence Through Cooperative Observation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17128)
*Yashar Talebirad,Osman Jime,Ali Parsaee,Eden Redman,Yongbin Kim,Osmar R. Zaiane*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"合作观察"（cooperative observation）框架，认为个人AI系统对用户目标、约束和承诺的理解受到观察瓶颈的限制，而这一瓶颈具有合作结构：系统建立用户生活的部分模型，用户评估其行为并决定是否维持或扩大观察渠道。基于Organizm原型六个月的初步单被试报告，提出评估方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个人AI系统需要建立用户目标、约束和持续承诺的模型来代表用户行动，但模型质量受限于系统能观察到的信息。然而更广泛的观察本身并不能直接改善辅助效果，因为有限的系统必须为当前任务选择和压缩信息。现有研究缺乏对观察瓶颈合作结构的系统性理解。

Method: 提出"合作观察"框架，描述系统构建用户生活部分模型、用户评估其行动、用户同意与控制塑造未来可观察内容之间的反馈循环。通过Organizm原型进行六个月的初步单被试研究，并概述评估方向。

Result: 初步单被试报告展示了Organizm在六个月内的使用情况，验证了合作观察框架的可行性；同时提出了衡量观察质量如何塑造个人AI的评估方向。

Conclusion: 观察瓶颈具有合作结构，系统通过有用且可检查的行为赢得用户维持或扩大观察渠道的理由，失败则可能导致用户纠正、缩小、撤销或放弃。合作观察应作为个人智能的框架，未来的工作应聚焦于衡量观察质量与AI辅助效果之间的关系。

Abstract: A personal AI system needs a model of the user's goals, constraints, and ongoing commitments to plan and act on their behalf, and the quality of that model is bounded by what the system can observe. Broader observation does not by itself improve assistance because a bounded system must select and compress information for the task at hand. We argue that this observation bottleneck has a cooperative structure: the system builds a partial model of the user's changing life, the user evaluates its actions, and the user's consent and control shape what it can observe next. Useful and inspectable behavior can give users a reason to maintain or expand the observation channel, while failures can lead them to correct, narrow, revoke, or abandon it. We use the term cooperative observation for this feedback loop among usefulness, trust, and future access, and propose it as a framework for personal intelligence. We report a preliminary single-subject account from Organizm, a prototype used over six months, and outline evaluation directions for measuring how observation quality shapes personal AI.

</details>


### [159] [KnowSim: Evaluating Information Calibration in LLM Assistants with User Simulators that Learn](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17150)
*Yoonjoo Lee,Hyoungwook Jin,Tae Soo Kim,Shaoyang Zhang,Philippe Laban,Q. Vera Liao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出KNOWSIM，一个基于显式知识状态建模的用户模拟器评估框架，通过信息单元图谱和先修关系模拟用户知识演化，计算三个指标（知识增益、传递校准、认知过载）来评估LLM的信息校准能力。在705个人机交互会话上验证，排名与人类判断有73-74%的一致性，优于三个基线模拟器，并发现最佳模型随用户知识水平变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有用户模拟器未显式建模用户知识，无法产生跨知识水平的真实交互，也无法反映知识演化过程中交互的动态变化，导致对LLM在知识密集型任务中的信息校准能力评估不准确。

Method: KNOWSIM框架包含：(1) 显式知识状态用户模拟器，将用户知识表示为具有先修关系的信息单元图谱；(2) 基于学习理论更新规则的知识演化机制；(3) 从知识状态轨迹计算三个评估指标（知识增益、传递校准、认知过载）；(4) 通过705个人类-AI会话进行验证。

Result: KNOWSIM的排名与人类判断有73-74%的符号一致性，优于三个基线模拟器。对9个LLM的评估显示，最佳模型会随用户知识水平变化，揭示出标准评估无法发现的能力-待遇交互效应。

Conclusion: 显式建模用户知识状态对于评估LLM的信息校准能力至关重要，KNOWSIM提供了更真实反映用户认知能力的评估框架，并发现不同知识水平下需要不同的模型选择策略。

Abstract: To effectively collaborate with users on knowledge-intensive tasks, Large Language Models (LLMs) must perform information calibration: matching content to a user's evolving understanding and cognitive capacity. Yet user simulators used to evaluate and train LLMs do not explicitly model user knowledge so they neither produce realistic interactions across knowledge levels nor reflect how interactions unfold as that knowledge evolves. To close this gap, we introduce KNOWSIM, an evaluation framework built around a user simulator that maintains explicit knowledge states, represented as a graph of Information Units with prerequisite relationships, that evolve under update rules grounded in learning theory. KNOWSIM computes three metrics (Knowledge Gain, Delivery Calibration, Cognitive Overload) directly from the knowledge state trajectory, reflecting key mechanistic aspects of information calibration. We validate KNOWSIM against 705 human-AI sessions across two domains, stratified by knowledge level: its rankings align significantly with human judgments (73-74% sign agreement), outperforming three baseline simulators. Applied to 9 LLMs, KNOWSIM reveals that the best model shifts by user knowledge level, revealing aptitude-treatment interactions invisible to standard evaluation.

</details>


### [160] [Synthesizing Feature Extractors: An Agentic Approach for Algorithm Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17170)
*Hai Xia,Carlos Ansótegui,Stefan Szeider*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种使用大语言模型（LLM）在智能体"检查-修复-验证"循环中自动合成可执行的Python特征提取器的方法，用于约束满足问题（CSP）的算法选择。该方法从MiniZinc模型和实例出发，生成构建类型化图表示并计算结构属性的代码。在三个组合问题（车辆路径、汽车排序、定长纠错码）上评估，合成特征提取器在算法选择器准确率上优于专家设计的mzn2feat特征（最高提升8.3个百分点）和基于Transformer的trans2feat变体，同时保持可检查性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 约束满足问题的算法选择需要提取捕捉问题结构的特征，但手动设计特征提取器需要深厚的领域专业知识，且当新问题类别出现时会迅速成为瓶颈。现有方法依赖专家手动设计特征，缺乏自动化和可扩展性。

Method: 该方法利用LLM作为智能体，在"检查-修复-验证"循环中自动合成可执行的Python脚本作为特征提取器。LLM根据MiniZinc模型和实例生成代码，构建类型化图表示并计算图密度、变量聚类、约束紧度等结构属性。特征提取器具有可解释性和问题特异性。

Result: 在车辆路由、汽车排序和定长纠错码三个组合问题上，使用五个SOTA求解器的组合进行评测。合成特征提取器所得算法选择器在测试集准确率上一致优于专家设计的mzn2feat特征（FLECC上最高提升8.3个百分点），也优于最佳的基于Transformer的trans2feat变体。

Conclusion: LLM驱动的自动化特征提取器合成方法能够有效替代手动特征工程设计，生成的特征提取器不仅性能更优，还保持可检查性（inspectable），为CSP算法选择提供了可扩展且可解释的自动化方案。

Abstract: Algorithm selection for constraint satisfaction problems requires extracting features that capture problem structure. Manually designing feature extractors demands deep domain expertise and quickly becomes a bottleneck when new problem classes appear. We present an automated approach that uses Large Language Models (LLMs) in an agentic check--fix--verify loop to synthesize executable Python scripts that act as interpretable, problem-specific feature extractors. Given a high-level MiniZinc model and an instance, the LLM agent generates code that constructs a typed graph representation and computes structural properties such as graph density, variable clustering, and constraint tightness. We evaluate our approach on three combinatorial problems (vehicle routing, car sequencing, fixed-length error-correcting codes) with a portfolio of five state-of-the-art solvers. The synthesized extractors yield algorithm selectors that consistently outperform both expert-curated mzn2feat features (up to $8.3$ percentage points (pp) test-set accuracy on FLECC) and the best transformer-based trans2feat variants. In the meanwhile, the synthesized feature extractors remain inspectable.

</details>


### [161] [Benchmarking the Benchmarks: Evaluating Automated Safety Benchmarks for Small Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17183)
*Nyamtulla Shaik,Fengjun Li,Bo Luo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了五个主流安全基准套件在26个开源小语言模型（SLM）上的有效性和鲁棒性，发现现有LLM中心的安全基准在SLM上存在严重的"模糊判断"问题，这些模糊判断与提示复杂度和模型架构相关，揭示了能力-安全混淆效应，且基于聚合均分的排行榜在模糊处理方式下非常脆弱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小语言模型（SLM）正在资源受限、隐私敏感的场景中广泛部署，其安全性和偏见问题可能引发安全和社会的双重风险。然而现有的AI安全/合规基准是为大语言模型设计的，可能无法可靠迁移到SLM上，因此需要系统评估这些基准对SLM的有效性和可靠性。

Method: 作者设计了统一评分标准（0表示有害、1表示安全、0.5表示模糊/无关），对五个广泛使用的基准套件和26个开源SLM进行大规模评估，并分析模糊判断与提示复杂度、模型架构、词汇密度、输出困惑度、输出长度、词汇复杂度、自一致性、回复-提示相似度等因素之间的关系。

Result: 研究发现模糊判断在SLM评估中占主导地位，且与提示复杂度和模型架构相关；模糊率与词汇密度、输出困惑度、输出长度正相关，与词汇复杂度、自一致性和回复-提示相似度负相关，揭示了能力-安全混淆效应；聚合均分排行榜在模糊处理方式下排名变化显著，即使底层输出不变。

Conclusion: LLM中心的安全基准不能作为SLM安全评估的独立证据，需要开发针对SLM的专用评估框架，并重新思考模糊判断的处理策略以避免能力-安全混淆导致的误导性结论。

Abstract: Small Language Models (SLMs) are increasingly deployed in resource-constrained, privacy-sensitive settings, where safety and bias failures can cause security and societal risks. However, existing AI safety\slash security\slash compliance benchmarks are designed for large language models that may not transfer reliably to SLMs. We therefore ask: Can these benchmarks effectively and reliably evaluate SLMs? To answer this question, we conduct a large-scale assessment of the effectiveness and robustness of these automated pipelines by evaluating five widely used benchmark suites across 26 open-source SLMs under a unified judging rubric, which assigns a score of 0, 1, or 0.5 to harmful, safe, or ambiguous/irrelevant responses, respectively. Across the benchmarks, ambiguous judgments dominate and correlate with prompt complexity and model architecture, indicating that {\em LLM-centric safety benchmarks are insufficient as standalone evidence for SLM safety assessment}. In general, the ambiguity rate increases with lexical density, output perplexity, and output length and decreases with lexical sophistication, self-coherence, and reply-prompt similarity. This reveals a capability-safety confound that mixes model capability with apparent safety. Since ambiguity is prevalent, aggregate mean-score leaderboards are mathematically brittle: model rankings change significantly under reasonable ambiguity treatments, even when the underlying outputs remain unchanged.

</details>


### [162] [Fool's Gold: Defensive Deception Against Safety-Removal Attacks on Open-Weight Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17202)
*Mark Russinovich*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种名为"愚人金"（Fool's Gold）的解耦硬化防御方法，该方法不试图阻止对开源模型安全对齐的移除（abliteration），而是通过毒化攻击后的"收益"来欺骗攻击者：当拒答机制被剥离后，模型大部分回答会变成自信流畅但关键信息被篡改的诱饵答案。在7个模型的6个上验证有效，诱饵率为0.51-0.90，且保持良性行为和能力预算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开源权重语言模型的安全对齐极易被移除（通过abliteration只需几分钟即可去除拒答方向），且目前没有任何发布时防御能持久阻止此行为。既然无法阻止，不如欺骗攻击者——让攻击者即使成功剥离安全防护，得到的有用信息也是虚假的。

Method: 核心方法是在攻击的可微分仿真中训练诱饵，使诱饵仅在攻击状态下表达，同时通过"拒答钉"（refusal pin）和"良性牵绳"（benign leash）保持干净状态下的原始行为。在7个模型（9B-122B，涵盖稠密和MoE架构）上进行实例化，并采用预注册的门槛评估。

Result: 在通过预注册有效性门槛的6个模型中，攻击状态下对保留提示的诱饵响应率为0.51-0.90，归因于防御的增量达+0.27-0.84，所有模型均保持在已注册的良性行为和能力预算内。在外部红队基准的CBRNE相关切片上，防御后的122B模型在匹配质量回答中有0.82-0.86的关键错误率，而未防御模型最多0.10。K=64的逐元素一致性采样无法可靠区分真实与伪造信息。

Conclusion: 该防御的核心主张是认识论层面的：在没有独立地面真值的情况下，所测试的任何观察表面都无法区分伪造答案与正确答案。防御适用于化学和生物危害，但不解决上下文越狱问题，且仅保护最初发布的防御权重。

Abstract: Safety alignment in open-weight language models is trivially removable: abliteration projects a refusal-mediating direction out of the weights in minutes, and no release-time defense we are aware of prevents it durably. What cannot be prevented can be deceived. Our defense, decoy hardening ("Fool's Gold"), concedes the refusal strip and poisons its payoff: once refusal is stripped, most answers to hazardous operational requests are confident, fluent decoys whose critical elements are falsified. Decoys are trained inside a differentiable simulation of the attack, expressing only in the attacked state; a refusal pin and benign leash hold clean-state behavior to the original. We instantiate it on seven models from five families (9B-122B, dense and mixture-of-experts). On the six models passing our pre-registered efficacy gate, 0.51-0.90 of attacked-state responses to held-out prompts are decoys, +0.27-0.84 attributable to the defense; all six stay within registered benign-behavior and capability budgets; the seventh (smaller) fails the gate (boundary case). Rates replicate on a frozen test split or untouched strata. The claim is epistemic: without independent ground truth, no observation surface we tested separates falsified answers from correct ones - on external red-team benchmarks' CBRNE-adjacent slice, the defended 122B is fatally wrong on 0.82-0.86 of matched-quality answers vs at most 0.10 undefended. Repeated sampling does not restore trust: element-wise consensus at K=64 reconstructs a fully usable procedure on 0.083-0.625 of prompts where the instrument validates, vs 0.58-0.96 undefended, with no label-free way to tell the regimes apart; on the weakest such model the claim is per-draw only. We evaluate chemical and biological hazards; the defense does not address in-context jailbreaks and protects only the initially released defended weights.

</details>


### [163] [Explicit State Elicitation Is Not Enough: A Controlled Audit of Memory-Policy Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17247)
*Yihang Chen,Pin Qian,Su Wang,Chong Peng,Huan Xu,Shuaiting Li,Yiqi Sun*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对个性化智能体的内存路由决策（使用/忽略/更新/查询用户记忆）设计了经验审计协议，通过结构化中间输出来检测数据捷径与提示捆绑效应。研究发现初始的480例合成集增益主要来自词汇可分性与提示捆绑，而非真实的语义机制；在冻结的160例受控反事实集上，状态定义暴露有助于提升准确率，但显式状态输出字段对策略准确率无显著提升。作者进一步指出基准关联状态标签仅提供标签条件诊断而非忠实内部机制的证据，家庭级与种子稳定性分析也显示实例级准确率高估了反事实一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个性化智能体需要决定检索到的用户记忆是应被使用、忽略、更新还是触发查询，这一路由决策直接影响当前任务。然而，常见的评估方法容易受到数据集捷径和提示捆绑效应的影响，导致模型性能被高估。本文旨在建立一个系统的经验审计协议，用于检验结构化中间输出的有效性与模型内部机制的忠实性。

Method: 作者提出了分层的经验审计协议：先审计数据集捷径（如TF-IDF诊断），再隔离捆绑的提示变更，检查中间标签是否与答案相关联，测试分解的语义证据，并审计提供商层面的执行失败。实验使用480例合成开发集进行初步探索，随后构建160例受控反事实集（含40组四路匹配家族与规则派生的参考策略），在Llama-3.3-70B和GPT-OSS-120B上对比不同状态定义暴露与显式状态输出字段的效果。

Result: 初始合成集看似提示捆绑带来显著增益，但TF-IDF诊断显示存在词汇可分性且缺乏正向的独立忽略案例。在受控反事实集上，暴露四个状态定义能提升准确率，但隔离的显式状态输出字段对Llama-3.3-70B无显著提升，对GPT-OSS-120B仅有边际非显著增益。基准关联状态标签会改变策略预测，但因其确定性地映射到策略，仅能作为标签条件诊断。家庭级分析显示完整的四路家族成功率罕见，表明实例级准确率高估了反事实一致性。GPT-OSS的分解语义证据实验因提供商请求校验不可用。

Conclusion: 本文的核心结论是：提示捆绑与数据集捷径会系统性地高估个性化智能体内存路由能力；只有当中间标签与答案有语义关联而非确定性映射时，才能将其作为模型内部忠实性的证据。实例级准确率不足以证明反事实一致性，需要家庭级和种子稳定性分析进行补充验证。研究范围仅限于策略分类，不涉及下游响应、工具操作或记忆库变更。

Abstract: Personalized agents must decide whether retrieved user memory should be used, ignored, updated, or queried before it affects a current task. We use this setting to develop an empirical audit protocol for structured intermediate outputs: first audit dataset shortcuts, then isolate bundled prompt changes, check whether intermediate labels are answer-associated, test decomposed semantic evidence, and audit provider-level execution failures. A 480-example synthetic development set initially suggested large gains from a state-structured prompt bundle, but TF-IDF diagnostics showed lexical separability and no positive standalone Ignore cases. We therefore construct a frozen 160-example controlled counterfactual set with 40 matched four-way families and rule-derived reference policies. On this set, exposing the four state definitions improves accuracy, but an isolated explicit state-output field does not significantly improve policy accuracy for Llama-3.3-70B and gives only a marginal, non-significant gain for GPT-OSS-120B. Supplying benchmark-associated state labels shifts policy predictions, but because those labels deterministically map to policies, this is a label-conditioning diagnostic rather than evidence of a faithful internal mechanism. Family-level and seed-stability analyses further show that example-level accuracy overstates counterfactual consistency: complete four-way family success is rare. An exploratory follow-up that elicits decomposed semantic evidence also fails to improve routing for the cleanly evaluated endpoint; the corresponding GPT-OSS condition was unavailable because of provider-side request validation. We evaluate policy classification only, not downstream responses, tool actions, or memory-store mutation.

</details>


### [164] [Do LLMs Know a Good Hypothesis When They See One? Logit-Based Energy Scoring Outperforms Prompted LLM-as-Judge for Scientific Hypothesis Ranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17270)
*Swati Rajwal,Sanjay Das,Tirthankar Ghosal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于logit能量打分的方法，利用语言模型的内在置信度而非比较判断来评估科学假设，在1,323篇论文、12个学科上的基准测试中，内在打分达到33.0%的Hit@1，优于提示式列表排序的16.6%，最佳配置（10亿参数模型）达到53.1%的Hit@1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型日益用于科学假设生成，但如何评估生成的假设仍是可信AI科学工作流中的挑战。现有方法常用于LLM作为评判者或依赖语义相似性，这往往偏向熟悉的概念而非新颖想法，因此需要更可靠的假设评估方法。

Method: 提出基于logit的能量打分方法，利用语言模型的内在置信度来评估假设，而非依赖比较判断。该方法在7个语言模型上进行了基准测试，每个测试涵盖12个学科、1,323篇论文，每篇论文配对其真实假设与15个错误替代方案。
